Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the monte_carlo object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 333. The moduli space has dimension at most 635 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to monte_carlo structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 333/635 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a monte_carlo algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 94. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 659/3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 94 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
